'yes' : 'no'`；Admin Managers 页面和保存/删除确认 snapshot 必须消费 `AdminManagerId`、`AdminManagerEditableSnapshotSystemRole=none/admin/super_admin` 与 `AdminManagerEditableSnapshotStatus=none/active/disabled`，不得回退 `id: string`、`editingAdminId: string | null` 或 `adminId: string | null`；Admin Managers 页面自定义角色勾选 `toggleRole(roleId)` 必须消费 `AdminRoleId`，不得回退 `roleId: string`；Admin Managers loading 分支和正文顶部必须展示该快照，管理员表格 unknown 系统角色必须展示 raw 值，编辑表单必须通过显式 mutable enum guard 提交，删除按钮必须只打开结构化确认弹窗，并通过确认动作调用既有 `adminManagersApi.delete()` 与列表 reload 链路，删除提交中必须锁定入口，避免管理员角色绑定或管理员删除状态只靠 loading 文案、错误 banner、表单显隐、角色 checkbox 数量、保存/删除按钮禁用或删除弹窗文案推断，也避免后端管理员枚举漂移被宽字符串误判为 active/admin
- Admin Manager 保存确认 snapshot 校验，包括 `AdminManagerSaveConfirmationSnapshotStatus` 必须显式覆盖 `closed/awaiting_confirmation/confirming/save_failed`，`AdminManagerSaveConfirmationSnapshotSource` 必须显式覆盖 `dialog_state/admin_save/role_binding`，`AdminManagerSaveConfirmationRiskLevel` 必须显式覆盖 `none/medium/high`，`AdminManagerSaveConfirmationSnapshot` 必须消费这些命名契约与 `AdminManagerId`，并覆盖管理员、系统角色、状态、自定义角色数量、保存中、错误、确认/取消能力、风险等级、说明、恢复建议和稳定 `admin-manager-save-confirmation-snapshot` UI target；Admin Managers 保存按钮必须只打开结构化确认弹窗，并通过确认动作调用既有 `adminManagersApi.update` 与列表 reload 链路，保存提交中必须锁定确认与取消，避免后台权限绑定变更继续以按钮直触发形式绕过 Snapshot Governance
- Admin LLM Providers page snapshot 校验，包括 `AdminLLMProvidersPageSnapshotStatus/Source`、`AdminLLMProviderFormMode` 与 `AdminLLMProviderReloadMessageState` 命名契约必须进入中央类型并被 builder 直接消费；Admin Layout 主导航 `NavItem.labelKey` 必须消费 `AdminLayoutNavLabelKey=dashboard/llm/prompts/config/audit/users/admins/roles`，不得在 Layout 局部重复维护 inline union；`AdminLLMProvidersPageSnapshot` 必须覆盖 `loading/load_failed/empty/ready/runtime_drift/reloading/reload_failed/creating/editing/form_incomplete/saving/save_failed/delete_confirming/delete_failed`、来源、provider 总数、启用数、运行态加载数、配置漂移数、默认 provider、当前运行 provider、创建/编辑模式、编辑/删除目标、表单长度、api key 输入存在性、reload 消息状态、last reload 时间、loading/saving/reloading、错误、创建/reload/保存/取消/确认删除能力、说明、恢复建议和稳定 `admin-llm-providers-page-snapshot` UI target；Admin LLM Providers loading 分支和正文顶部必须展示该快照；页面层 provider summary、默认 provider、reload/last reload 文案、页面错误、空列表、默认 badge、runtime drift 提示、设默认按钮、表单弹层和确认弹窗文案必须先派生 `getAdminLLMProviderDisplayLabel()`、`getAdminLLMProviderReloadMessageClassName()`、`shouldRenderAdminLLMProvidersPageError()`、`shouldRenderAdminLLMProvidersEmptyState()`、`shouldRenderAdminLLMProviderRuntimeDriftNotice()`、`shouldRenderAdminLLMProviderSetDefaultAction()` 与命名 label facts；不得回退 `setDefaultProviderName(data.default_name || '')`、`setReloadMessage(result.message || ...)`、`result.providers?.length || 0`、`if (editingProvider)`、`enabled: !provider.enabled`、`{error && ...}`、`{providers.length === 0 && ...}`、`{provider.is_default && ...}`、`{provider.enabled && !provider.runtime_loaded && ...}`、`{!provider.is_default && provider.enabled && ...}`、`{showForm && ...}`、`reloadMessage && !reloadMessage.includes('失败')`、`defaultProviderName || '未设置'`、`lastReloadAt || '暂无'` 或 `provider.display_name/model/base_url || ...`，避免 provider 管理页面状态只靠列表卡片、runtime 统计卡、reload 文案、漂移提示、表单弹窗、删除确认或按钮禁用推断
- Admin LLM Provider 保存确认 snapshot 校验，包括 `AdminLLMProviderSaveConfirmationSnapshotStatus/Source`、`AdminLLMProviderConfirmationRiskLevel` 与 `AdminLLMProviderType` 命名契约必须进入中央类型并被 builder 直接消费；`AdminLLMProviderSaveConfirmationSnapshot` 必须覆盖 `closed/awaiting_confirmation/confirming/save_failed`、来源、create/edit 模式、provider id/name/display name、类型、base_url/model 长度、API Key 输入或保留、启用/默认意图、priority、保存中、错误、确认/取消能力、风险等级、说明、恢复建议和稳定 `admin-llm-provider-save-confirmation-snapshot` UI target；Admin LLM Providers 的创建/编辑保存按钮必须只打开结构化确认弹窗，并通过确认动作调用既有 `createProvider/updateProvider` 与 reload 链路，保存提交中必须锁定入口；Admin LLM Provider API 读写契约、页面 select cast、form snapshot input 与保存确认 `providerType` 必须统一消费 `AdminLLMProviderType`，禁止回退 `cloud/local` inline union、`type?: string` 或 `providerType: string`，避免 provider 配置写入继续以表单按钮直触发形式绕过 Snapshot Governance，也避免 Provider 类型在 API、页面与 snapshot 边界漂移
- Admin LLM Provider 删除确认 snapshot 校验，包括 `AdminLLMProviderDeleteConfirmationSnapshotStatus/Source` 与 `AdminLLMProviderConfirmationRiskLevel` 命名契约必须进入中央类型并被 builder 直接消费；`AdminLLMProviderDeleteConfirmationSnapshot` 必须覆盖 `closed/awaiting_confirmation/confirming/delete_failed`、来源、provider id/name/display name、默认 provider、enabled、runtime_loaded、runtime_active、删除提交中、错误、确认/取消能力、风险等级、说明、恢复建议和稳定 `admin-llm-provider-delete-confirmation-snapshot` UI target；Admin LLM Providers 删除弹窗必须展示该快照并通过 `canConfirm/canCancel` 门控确认与取消，删除请求提交中必须锁定入口，避免后台 provider 删除状态、运行态影响和失败恢复只靠普通 AlertDialog 文案或页面级按钮禁用推断
- Admin LLM Provider 运行态变更确认 snapshot 校验，包括 `AdminLLMProviderRuntimeMutationConfirmationSnapshotStatus/Source`、`AdminLLMProviderRuntimeMutationAction/Kind` 与 `AdminLLMProviderConfirmationRiskLevel` 命名契约必须进入中央类型并被 builder 直接消费；`AdminLLMProviderRuntimeMutationConfirmationSnapshot` 必须覆盖 `closed/awaiting_confirmation/confirming/mutation_failed`、来源、`toggle_enabled/set_default` action、provider id/name/display name、默认 provider、enabled、nextEnabled、runtime_loaded、runtime_active、提交中、错误、确认/取消能力、风险等级、说明、恢复建议和稳定 `admin-llm-provider-runtime-mutation-confirmation-snapshot` UI target；Admin LLM Providers 的启用/禁用和设为默认按钮必须只打开结构化确认弹窗，并通过统一 dispatcher 在确认后调用既有 `updateProvider/setDefault` 与 reload 链路，提交中必须锁定入口，避免 provider 运行态配置变更继续以按钮直触发形式绕过 Snapshot Governance
- Admin Users page snapshot 与 status/role/soft-delete confirmation contract 校验，包括 `src/lib/admin/api.ts` 必须显式定义 `AdminUserStatus=active/disabled/deleted/unknown`、`AdminUserRole=user/admin/super_admin/unknown`、`AdminUserMutableStatus=active/disabled` 与 `AdminUserMutableRole=user/admin/super_admin`，`AdminUserRawResponse` 必须显式列出后端 User model raw 字段并禁止通过 `Omit<AdminUser,...>` 从 normalized user 反推，`adminUsersApi.list()` 必须把后端 raw `status/role` 归一为这些稳定枚举并保留 `raw_status/raw_role`，用户更新输入不得接受 `unknown/deleted` 且不得通过 `Exclude<...,'unknown'>` 反推可写枚举；`AdminUsersPageSnapshotStatus` 必须显式覆盖 `loading/load_failed/empty/ready/editing/saving/save_failed/delete_confirming/deleting/delete_failed`，`AdminUsersPageSnapshotSource` 必须显式覆盖 `user_list/user_status/user_role/user_edit/user_save/user_delete`，`AdminUserEditingId`、`AdminUserDeletingId`、`AdminUserEditableSnapshotStatus=none/active/disabled`、`AdminUserEditableSnapshotRole=none/user/admin/super_admin`、`AdminUserDeleteSnapshotStatus=none/active/disabled/deleted/unknown`、`AdminUserSaveConfirmationSnapshotStatus=closed/awaiting_confirmation/confirming/save_failed`、`AdminUserSaveConfirmationSnapshotSource=dialog_state/user_save/role_update/status_update`、`AdminUserSaveConfirmationRiskLevel=none/medium/high`、`AdminUserDeleteConfirmationSnapshotStatus=closed/awaiting_confirmation/confirming/delete_failed`、`AdminUserDeleteConfirmationSnapshotSource=dialog_state/user_delete` 与 `AdminUserDeleteConfirmationRiskLevel=none/high` 必须进入中央类型并被 builder 直接消费；`AdminUsersPageSnapshot` 必须覆盖用户总数、启用用户数、禁用用户数、deleted 用户数、未知状态数、未知 role 数、未知 status/role raw 值、具名用户数、admin/super_admin role 数量、当前编辑用户、当前删除用户、目标 role/status、loading、saving、deleting、错误、编辑/保存/取消/删除/刷新能力、说明、恢复建议和稳定 `admin-users-page-snapshot` UI target；用户 status/role 计数、unknown status/role raw value 收集、具名用户计数和 admin/super_admin role 计数必须通过 `collectUniqueValues()`、`countAdminUsersByStatus()`、`countAdminUsersByRole()`、`collectAdminUsersUnknownStatusValues()`、`collectAdminUsersUnknownRoleValues()`、`countNamedAdminUsers()` 与 `countAdminUsersWithAdminRole()` 显式 `for...of` reader 派生，不得回退 `values.map(...).filter(...)`、`users.filter(...)` 或 unknown users `.map(...)`；Admin Users 页面、保存确认和删除确认快照的 unknown raw values、editing/deleting/user/email fallback 与 yes-no 展示必须先派生 `getAdminUsersSnapshotLabel()`、`getAdminUsersUnknownValueListLabel()`、`unknownStatusValueLabel`、`unknownRoleValueLabel`、`editingUserIdLabel`、`deletingUserIdLabel`、`userIdLabel`、`userEmailLabel`、`isLoadingLabel`、`isSavingLabel`、`isDeletingLabel`、`hasErrorLabel`、`canStartEditLabel`、`canConfirmLabel` 及对应 display labels，不得回退 `join(' / ') || 'none'`、`snapshot.userId || 'none'`、`snapshot.userEmail || 'none'`、`editing?.email ?? ''`、`pendingDelete?.email ?? ''` 或 JSX inline `snapshot.* ? 'yes' : 'no'`；`AdminUserSaveConfirmationSnapshot` 必须覆盖用户身份、邮箱、目标 role/status、保存中、错误、确认/取消能力、风险等级、说明、恢复建议和稳定 `admin-user-save-confirmation-snapshot` UI target；`AdminUserDeleteConfirmationSnapshot` 必须覆盖用户身份、邮箱、当前 status、删除中、错误、确认/取消能力、风险等级、说明、恢复建议和稳定 `admin-user-delete-confirmation-snapshot` UI target；Admin Users loading 分支和正文顶部必须展示页面快照，Users 表格 unknown 状态、deleted 状态和 unknown role 必须展示显式值，编辑表单必须通过显式 mutable enum guard 提交，保存按钮和删除按钮必须只打开结构化确认弹窗，并分别通过确认动作调用既有 `adminUsersApi.update()` / `adminUsersApi.delete()` 与列表 reload 链路，保存或删除提交中必须锁定入口，已 `deleted` 用户不得重复触发删除，避免用户管理页面状态只靠 loading 文案、错误 banner、空表格行、状态 badge、用户名列、刷新按钮文案或按钮禁用推断，也避免后端用户枚举漂移被宽字符串误判为启用、禁用、deleted、普通角色或管理员
- Admin RBAC snapshot list contract 校验，包括 `workspace-types.ts` 必须显式导出 `AdminPermissionCodeList`、`AdminPermissionIdList`、`AdminRoleIdList` 与 `AdminUnknownRawValueList`；Admin layout/dashboard profile input、Admin layout nav permission requirements、Roles form input、Managers editing input、Users unknown raw value collector 以及 Roles/Managers/Users snapshot 中的 unknown raw enum 字段必须直接消费这些命名 list contract；validator 必须禁止 `permission_codes: string[]`、`requiredPermissions?: string[]`、`permission_ids: string[]`、`role_ids: string[]`、`unknownStatusValues: string[]`、`unknownSystemRoleValues: string[]` 与 `unknownRoleValues: string[]` 回退，避免权限绑定列表、导航权限要求和用户可见 raw enum 值列表继续以匿名数组漂移
- Admin Audit page snapshot 校验，包括 `AdminAuditPageSnapshotStatus/Source` 命名契约必须进入中央类型并被 builder 直接消费；`AdminAuditPageSnapshot` 必须覆盖 `loading/load_failed/empty/ready/invalid_timestamp`、来源、审计日志总数、action 种类数、target_type 种类数、IP 数量、缺失目标数量、非法时间戳数量、最近 action、最近 target_type、loading、错误、刷新能力、说明、恢复建议和稳定 `admin-audit-page-snapshot` UI target；页面快照输入日志和唯一值计数 helper 必须消费 `AdminAuditLogSnapshotInputList` 与 `AdminAuditSnapshotValueList`，不得回退匿名数组；unique action/target_type/IP 计数、缺失 target 计数、非法 timestamp 计数和最新日志读取必须通过 `countUnique()`、`countUniqueAdminAuditLogValues()`、`countAdminAuditLogsWithMissingTarget()`、`countAdminAuditLogsWithInvalidTimestamp()` 与 `getAdminAuditPageSnapshotLatestLog()` 显式 `for...of` reader 派生，不得回退 `values.filter(...)`、`logs.map(...)`、`logs.filter(...)` 或 `logs[0]`；`AuditLog.id/admin_id/target_id` 必须消费 `AdminAuditLogId`、`AdminAuditActorAdminId` 与 `AdminAuditTargetId`，页面快照输入 `target_id` 必须消费 `AdminAuditTargetId`，不得回退裸 `number/string`；latest action/target fallback 与 loading/error/reload yes-no 展示必须先通过本地 label helpers 派生命名 display labels，不得回退 `latestLog?.action || 'none'`、`latestLog?.target_type || 'none'` 或 `snapshot.* ? 'yes' : 'no'` inline display gate；Admin Audit loading 分支和正文顶部必须展示该快照，避免审计日志页面状态只靠 loading 文案、错误 banner、空表格行、action badge、target 文本、时间列或刷新按钮文案推断，也避免审计日志、操作者和动态目标身份在 API 与 snapshot 边界漂移
- Admin Project read-side identity contract 校验，包括 `src/lib/admin/api.ts` 必须显式定义 `AdminProjectRecordId`、`AdminProjectId` 与 `AdminProjectOwnerUserId`，`AdminProject.id/project_id/user_id` 必须直接消费这些命名开放身份 contract，不得回退裸 `string`；Admin Dashboard 和 Admin Runtime Health 诊断继续只消费 `/api/admin/projects` 只读项目索引，不得因身份收口扩大为项目写入、runtime inspect、preview token 签发或状态写入，避免 Admin 运维观测项目索引在项目记录、项目和归属用户身份上漂移
- Admin LLM Provider identity contract 校验，包括 `src/lib/admin/api.ts` 必须显式定义 `AdminLLMProviderId` 并复用 `AIModelProvider` 承载 provider name；`LLMProvider.id`、`AdminLLMProvidersResponse.default_id`、`adminLLMApi.getProvider/updateProvider/deleteProvider/setDefault`、Admin LLM 页面删除 handler、Admin LLM Provider 管理快照、Admin Provider Health summary 与 Admin Dashboard provider priority issue helper 必须直接消费该命名 provider 身份 contract，不得回退裸 `number` 或 `number | string`；`LLMProvider.name`、`AdminLLMProvidersResponse.default_name`、`LLMRuntimeProviderInfo.name` 与 `LLMProviderCreate.name` 必须直接消费 `AIModelProvider`，不得回退裸字符串；该身份收口不得改变 Provider 列表、详情读取、配置更新、删除、默认设置、runtime reload 或诊断派生语义，避免 Provider 管理页面和 Admin 诊断模型之间的 provider 身份漂移
- Admin System Config identity/key contract 校验，包括 `src/lib/admin/api.ts` 必须显式定义 `AdminSystemConfigId` 与 `AdminSystemConfigKey`，`SystemConfig.id/key`、`adminConfigApi.update(key, ...)`、Admin Config 页面 `editingKey` state、保存 handler、Admin Config page snapshot 与 save confirmation snapshot 必须直接消费这些命名配置项身份和写入目标 key contract，不得回退裸 `number/string`；该身份收口不得改变配置列表读取、按 key 更新、权限判断、Prompt/Container 配置风险识别或保存确认语义，避免系统配置治理继续从宽字符串 key 派生
- Admin Prompt Config key contract 校验，包括 `src/lib/admin/api.ts` 必须显式定义 `AdminPromptConfigKey`，Admin Prompt 页面 profile key、`editingKey` state、保存 handler、Admin Prompt page snapshot 与 save confirmation snapshot 必须直接消费该命名 Prompt 写入目标 key contract，不得回退裸 `string`；该收口不得改变 Prompt 配置列表筛选、已知 Prompt profile 展示、按 key 保存或运行期 Prompt 注入语义，避免 prompt.* 管理页面继续从宽字符串 key 派生
- Admin Enterprise SSO Config key contract 校验，包括 `src/lib/admin/api.ts` 必须显式定义 `AdminEnterpriseSsoConfigKey` 与 `AdminEnterpriseSsoConfigKeyList`，Admin Enterprise Governance 页面的 `ADMIN_ENTERPRISE_SSO_CONFIG_KEYS`、必填配置列表、配置 value map、readiness 派生和页面快照必须直接消费该命名 SSO 配置 key contract，不得回退裸 `string[]` 或宽字符串 readiness；该收口不得改变 Admin Config API 读写语义，也不得把 SSO 配置存在误判为登录回调已接线
- Admin Enterprise Organization / Team / Member readiness / mutation contract 校验，包括 `src/lib/admin/api.ts` 必须显式定义 `AdminEnterpriseOrganizationReadinessStatus=schema_ready_no_data/team_ready_no_members/member_ready`、`AdminEnterpriseOrganizationReadinessResponse`、`AdminEnterpriseOrganization`、`AdminEnterpriseOrganizationCreateInput`、`AdminEnterpriseOrganizationStatus=active/disabled`、`AdminEnterpriseTeam`、`AdminEnterpriseTeamCreateInput`、`AdminEnterpriseTeamStatus=active/disabled`、`AdminEnterpriseMember`、`AdminEnterpriseMemberBindInput`、`AdminEnterpriseMemberRole=member` 与 `AdminEnterpriseMemberStatus=active/disabled`，`adminEnterpriseApi.getOrganizationReadiness()` 必须只读 `/enterprise/organization-readiness`，`adminEnterpriseApi.listOrganizations()` 必须只读 `/enterprise/organizations`，`adminEnterpriseApi.listTeams()` 必须只读 `/enterprise/teams`，`adminEnterpriseApi.createOrganization()` 必须只 POST `/enterprise/organizations`，`adminEnterpriseApi.createTeam()` 必须只 POST `/enterprise/teams`，`adminEnterpriseApi.bindMember()` 必须只 POST `/enterprise/members`；Admin Enterprise Governance snapshot 必须消费 `AdminEnterpriseOrganizationGovernanceReadinessStatus`，组织、团队、成员计数必须进入稳定快照条，组织、团队与成员绑定表单必须 super_admin gated 且成功后刷新 readiness，团队创建必须绑定已有 organization_id，成员绑定必须绑定已有 organization_id、team_id 和 user_id，不得用裸 `string`、truthy 判断或用户数量反推组织治理 readiness，也不得借组织、团队或成员绑定扩展项目归属、租户隔离或组织级 RBAC
- Admin Enterprise Project Ownership readiness contract 校验，包括 `src/lib/admin/api.ts` 必须显式定义 `AdminEnterpriseProjectOwnershipReadinessStatus=no_projects/organization_model_not_ready/legacy_user_owned/ownership_schema_ready` 与 `AdminEnterpriseProjectOwnershipReadinessResponse`，`adminEnterpriseApi.getProjectOwnershipReadiness()` 必须只读 `/enterprise/project-ownership-readiness`；Next proxy 必须转发到 `/api/admin/enterprise/project-ownership-readiness`；后端 handler 必须 super_admin-only；后端必须显式建模 `EnterpriseProjectOwnership` / `enterprise_project_ownerships` 并通过 GORM/Supabase `CountEnterpriseProjectOwnerships()` 读取映射计数；Admin Enterprise Governance snapshot 必须消费 `AdminEnterpriseProjectOwnershipGovernanceReadinessStatus` 并展示 project_count、legacy_user_owned_project_count、organization_project_count、unmigrated_project_count 与 readiness status，不得从 Admin Projects 列表长度、组织数量或成员数量反推项目归属迁移状态，也不得把 `ownership_schema_ready` 误判为租户隔离、组织级 RBAC 或 owner guard 已接线
- Admin Enterprise Project Ownership preflight contract 校验，包括 `src/lib/admin/api.ts` 必须显式定义 `AdminEnterpriseProjectOwnershipPreflightStatus=no_projects/organization_model_not_ready/no_candidates/candidate_ready`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectOwnershipPreflightCandidate` 与 `AdminEnterpriseProjectOwnershipPreflightResponse`，候选身份必须消费 `AdminProjectRecordId/AdminProjectId/AdminProjectOwnerUserId`；`adminEnterpriseApi.getProjectOwnershipPreflight()` 必须只读 `/enterprise/project-ownership-preflight`；Next proxy 必须转发到 `/api/admin/enterprise/project-ownership-preflight`；后端 handler 必须 super_admin-only；后端必须通过 GORM/Supabase `ListEnterpriseProjectOwnerships()` 读取显式映射，并通过 `ProjectRepo.ListAll(ctx, 1, 50)` 派生未映射项目候选；Admin Enterprise Governance snapshot 必须消费 `AdminEnterpriseProjectOwnershipGovernancePreflightStatus` 并展示 candidate_project_count、existing_ownership_count 与 preflight status；该入口不得执行迁移、不得写 `projects`、不得写 `enterprise_project_ownerships`、不得改变 owner guard、不得启用租户隔离或组织级 RBAC
- Admin Enterprise Project Ownership migration contract 校验，包括 `src/lib/admin/api.ts` 必须显式定义 `AdminEnterpriseProjectOwnershipStatus=active`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectOwnershipMigrationStatus=migrated`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectOwnership`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectOwnershipMigrateInput` 与 `AdminEnterpriseProjectOwnershipMigrationResult`，迁移目标必须消费 `AdminProjectRecordId/AdminEnterpriseOrganizationId/AdminEnterpriseTeamId`；`adminEnterpriseApi.migrateProjectOwnership()` 必须只 POST `/enterprise/project-ownership-migrations`；Next proxy 必须转发到 `/api/admin/enterprise/project-ownership-migrations` 并使用 `bodyMode=json`；后端 handler 必须 super_admin-only；后端必须要求 `confirm_migrate=true`，校验项目存在、组织存在、可选团队存在且归属组织、项目尚未存在显式映射后，只写入 `enterprise_project_ownerships` 并写入 `migrate_enterprise_project_ownership` Admin audit；Admin Enterprise 页面必须在预检卡片内展示显式确认表单，成功后刷新 readiness/preflight；该入口不得写 `projects`、不得改变 owner guard、不得启用租户隔离或组织级 RBAC
- Admin Enterprise Project Ownership mappings readback contract 校验，包括 `src/lib/admin/api.ts` 必须显式定义 `AdminEnterpriseProjectOwnershipMappingStatus=no_mappings/mapping_ready`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectOwnershipMapping` 与 `AdminEnterpriseProjectOwnershipMappingsResponse`，映射、项目、组织、团队身份必须消费 `AdminProjectRecordId/AdminProjectId/AdminProjectOwnerUserId/AdminEnterpriseOrganizationId/AdminEnterpriseTeamId`；`adminEnterpriseApi.getProjectOwnershipMappings()` 必须只读 `/enterprise/project-ownership-mappings`；Next proxy 必须转发到 `/api/admin/enterprise/project-ownership-mappings`；后端 handler 必须 super_admin-only；后端必须通过 `ListEnterpriseProjectOwnerships()`、`ListEnterpriseOrganizations()`、`ListEnterpriseTeams()` 和 `ProjectRepo.FindByID()` 合并映射证据，返回 ownership_count、returned_mapping_count、missing_project_count、mapping_limit、mappings 与 mapping_status；Admin Enterprise Governance snapshot 必须消费 `AdminEnterpriseProjectOwnershipGovernanceMappingStatus` 并展示 mapping count、missing project count 与 mapping status；该入口不得执行迁移、不得写 `projects`、不得写 `enterprise_project_ownerships`、不得改变 owner guard、不得启用租户隔离或组织级 RBAC
- Admin Enterprise Project Ownership owner guard readiness contract 校验，包括 `src/lib/admin/api.ts` 必须显式定义 `AdminEnterpriseProjectOwnershipOwnerGuardReadinessStatus=no_projects/no_mappings/unmapped_projects/mapping_evidence_drift/owner_guard_ready`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectOwnershipOwnerGuardReadinessCandidate` 与 `AdminEnterpriseProjectOwnershipOwnerGuardReadinessResponse`，项目身份必须消费 `AdminProjectRecordId/AdminProjectId/AdminProjectOwnerUserId`；`adminEnterpriseApi.getProjectOwnershipOwnerGuardReadiness()` 必须只读 `/enterprise/project-ownership-owner-guard-readiness`；Next proxy 必须转发到 `/api/admin/enterprise/project-ownership-owner-guard-readiness`；后端 handler 必须 super_admin-only；后端必须通过 `ListEnterpriseProjectOwnerships()` 和分页 `ProjectRepo.ListAll(ctx, page, 100)` 比对显式映射覆盖率，返回 project_count、ownership_count、mapped_project_count、unmapped_project_count、extra_ownership_count、preview_limit、unmapped_projects 与 owner_guard_status；Admin Enterprise Governance snapshot 必须消费 `AdminEnterpriseProjectOwnershipGovernanceOwnerGuardReadinessStatus` 并展示 mapped/unmapped/extra owner guard count 与 owner guard status；该入口不得执行迁移、不得写 `projects`、不得写 `enterprise_project_ownerships`、不得修改 `requireOwnedProject` 或 `workspace_project_access`、不得启用租户隔离或组织级 RBAC
- Admin Enterprise Project Access Guard switch readiness contract 校验，包括 `src/lib/admin/api.ts` 必须显式定义 `AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardSwitchReadinessStatus=ownership_repo_unavailable/no_projects/no_mappings/unmapped_projects/mapping_evidence_drift/enterprise_switch_ready`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardMode=legacy_user_owned/enterprise_owned` 与 `AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardSwitchReadinessResponse`；`adminEnterpriseApi.getProjectAccessGuardSwitchReadiness()` 必须只读 `/enterprise/project-access-guard-switch-readiness`；Next proxy 必须转发到 `/api/admin/enterprise/project-access-guard-switch-readiness`；后端 handler 必须 super_admin-only；后端必须通过 `AdminConsoleService.GetEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardSwitchReadiness()` 复用服务层 `ProjectService.GetProjectAccessGuardEnterpriseSwitchReadiness()`，返回 status、current_mode、target_mode、can_switch_to_enterprise_owned、project_count、ownership_count、mapped_project_count、unmapped_project_count、extra_ownership_count、ownership_lookup_available、enterprise_authorization_active、message 与 recovery；Admin Enterprise Governance snapshot 必须消费 `AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardGovernanceSwitchReadinessStatus` 并展示 mapped/unmapped/extra switch count、can switch、authorization active 与 switch status；该入口不得执行迁移、不得写 `projects`、不得写 `enterprise_project_ownerships`、不得修改 `AuthorizeProjectAccess()`、不得启用租户隔离或组织级 RBAC
- Enterprise Project Access Guard enterprise authorization dry-run contract 校验，包括后端必须显式定义 `ProjectAccessEnterpriseAuthorizationDryRunStatus=unavailable/no_mapping/membership_lookup_failed/no_active_membership/membership_ready` 与 `ProjectAccessEnterpriseAuthorizationDriftStatus=not_compared/aligned/enterprise_unavailable/legacy_granted_enterprise_blocked/legacy_blocked_enterprise_granted`，`ProjectAccessDecision` 必须携带 `EnterpriseAuthorizationDryRunStatus`、`EnterpriseAuthorizationDryRunDecision` 与 `EnterpriseAuthorizationDriftStatus`；`AuthorizeProjectAccess()` 必须先完成 legacy `projects.user_id` 真实判定，再调用 dry-run 附加 evidence，不得让 dry-run grant 改变 `HasProjectAccess()`；dry-run 必须通过 `ProjectAccessEnterpriseOwnershipRepo.FindEnterpriseMembersByUserAndOrganizationID()` 只读查询 `enterprise_members`，只接受 active 成员，可选 team-owned project 必须要求同 team 或 organization-level membership；GORM/Supabase Admin repo 必须实现成员查询；测试必须覆盖 aligned、legacy granted enterprise blocked、legacy blocked enterprise granted、membership lookup failure、no mapping、inactive member、team scope 和 org-level membership。该 dry-run 当前不得把 `enterprise_project_ownerships` 或 `enterprise_members` 作为真实访问控制事实、不得写 `projects`、不得启用租户隔离或组织级 RBAC
- Admin Enterprise Project Access Guard authorization dry-run evidence contract 校验，包括 `src/lib/admin/api.ts` 必须显式定义 `AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardAuthorizationDryRunEvidenceStatus=ownership_repo_unavailable/no_projects/enterprise_unavailable/drift_detected/dry_run_aligned`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardAuthorizationDryRunStatus`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardAuthorizationDriftStatus`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessDecisionStatus`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardAuthorizationDryRunDriftCandidate` 与 `AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardAuthorizationDryRunEvidenceResponse`；`adminEnterpriseApi.getProjectAccessGuardAuthorizationDryRunEvidence()` 必须只读 `/enterprise/project-access-guard-authorization-dry-run`；Next proxy 必须转发到 `/api/admin/enterprise/project-access-guard-authorization-dry-run`；后端 handler 必须 super_admin-only；后端必须通过 `AdminConsoleService.GetEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardAuthorizationDryRunEvidence()` 复用服务层 dry-run aggregation，返回 status、current_mode、target_mode、project_count、compared/aligned/unavailable/drift count、drift_candidates、enterprise_authorization_active、message 与 recovery；Admin Enterprise Governance snapshot 必须消费 `AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardGovernanceAuthorizationDryRunStatus` 并展示 dry-run compared/aligned/unavailable/drift count、candidate count、authorization active 与 dry-run status；该入口不得执行迁移、不得写 `projects`、不得写 `enterprise_project_ownerships`、不得修改 `AuthorizeProjectAccess()` 真实授权、不得启用租户隔离或组织级 RBAC
- Admin Enterprise Project Access Guard activation readiness contract 校验，包括后端必须显式定义 `ProjectAccessGuardEnterpriseActivationReadinessStatus=ownership_repo_unavailable/no_projects/switch_not_ready/dry_run_unavailable/drift_detected/already_active/ready_to_activate`、`ProjectAccessGuardEnterpriseActivationBlockerSource=switch_unmapped_project/switch_extra_ownership/dry_run_enterprise_unavailable/dry_run_authorization_drift`、`ProjectAccessGuardEnterpriseActivationReviewItemSource=switch_readiness/authorization_dry_run/blocker_candidates/authorization_mode/tenant_isolation_boundary/organization_rbac_boundary/manual_activation_task`、`ProjectAccessGuardEnterpriseActivationReviewItemStatus=passed/blocked/manual_required`、`ProjectAccessGuardEnterpriseActivationAuditPlanItemSource=readiness_snapshot/blocker_snapshot/manual_approval/activation_execution/post_activation_access_validation/rollback_evidence`、`ProjectAccessGuardEnterpriseActivationAuditPlanItemStatus=evidence_ready/blocked/manual_required`、`ProjectAccessGuardEnterpriseActivationReadiness`、`ProjectAccessGuardEnterpriseActivationBlockerCandidate`、`ProjectAccessGuardEnterpriseActivationReviewItem` 与 `ProjectAccessGuardEnterpriseActivationAuditPlanItem`；`ProjectService.GetProjectAccessGuardEnterpriseActivationReadiness()` 必须复用 `GetProjectAccessGuardEnterpriseSwitchReadiness()` 与 `GetProjectAccessGuardEnterpriseAuthorizationDryRunEvidence()` 合成只读 readiness，只有 switch readiness ready、dry-run aligned、current mode 仍为 `legacy_user_owned`、无 blocker candidates 且 `EnterpriseAuthorizationActive=false` 时才能让 `HasEnterpriseActivationReadiness()` 为 true；当 readiness 被 switch 或 dry-run 阻断时，必须只读暴露最多 50 条 blocker candidates，覆盖未映射项目、额外映射、enterprise unavailable 和授权漂移；readiness 必须派生只读 review items，核心检查可为 passed/blocked，tenant isolation、organization RBAC 和 manual activation task 必须显式保持 manual_required；readiness 还必须派生只读 audit plan items，readiness snapshot 和 blocker snapshot 可为 evidence_ready/blocked，manual approval、activation execution、post-activation access validation 和 rollback evidence 必须显式保持 manual_required，且不得写 audit 记录；`src/lib/admin/api.ts` 必须显式定义 `AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationReadinessStatus`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationBlockerSource`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationReviewItemSource`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationReviewItemStatus`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationAuditPlanItemSource`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationAuditPlanItemStatus`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationBlockerCandidate`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationReviewItem`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationAuditPlanItem` 与 `AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationReadinessResponse`；`adminEnterpriseApi.getProjectAccessGuardActivationReadiness()` 必须只读 `/enterprise/project-access-guard-activation-readiness`；Next proxy 必须转发到 `/api/admin/enterprise/project-access-guard-activation-readiness`；后端 handler 必须 super_admin-only；后端必须通过 `AdminConsoleService.GetEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationReadiness()` 复用服务层 aggregation，返回 status、current_mode、target_mode、can_activate_enterprise_owned、switch_status、authorization_dry_run_status、映射覆盖计数、dry-run 比对计数、authorization_drift_count、blocker_preview_limit、blocker_candidates、review_items、audit_plan_items、enterprise_authorization_active、message 与 recovery；Admin Enterprise Governance snapshot 必须消费 `AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardGovernanceActivationReadinessStatus` 并展示 activation can activate、switch status、dry-run status、mapped/unmapped/extra count、compared/aligned/unavailable/drift count、blocker candidate count、review item count、review blocked count、review manual-required count、audit plan item count、audit plan blocked count、audit plan manual-required count、authorization active 与 activation status；该入口不得执行迁移、不得写 `projects`、不得写 `enterprise_project_ownerships`、不得写 audit 记录、不得修改 `AuthorizeProjectAccess()` 真实授权、不得启用 enterprise_owned 授权、租户隔离或组织级 RBAC
- Admin Enterprise Project Access Guard activation audit readback readiness contract 校验，包括后端必须定义 `EnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationAudit` 模型并映射 `enterprise_project_access_guard_activation_audits`；fresh init SQL、`backend/init.sql`、GORM AutoMigrate、GORM/Supabase `CountEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationAudits(ctx)`、`ListEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationAudits(ctx, limit)` 与 service_role-only RLS 必须共同固定 schema 和只读回读；Supabase readback 必须映射 `readiness_snapshot/blocker_snapshot/review_snapshot/audit_plan_snapshot/execution_result/rollback_reference`；后端必须暴露 super_admin-only `GET /api/admin/enterprise/project-access-guard-activation-audit-readiness`，返回 `audit_event_count`、`required_event_type_count`、`missing_required_event_type_count`、`recent_event_limit`、`payload_integrity_status`、`payload_integrity_issue_count`、`payload_integrity_issue_limit`、`payload_integrity_issues`、`metadata_integrity_status`、`metadata_integrity_issue_count`、`metadata_integrity_issue_limit`、`metadata_integrity_issues`、`required_event_items`、`recent_events`、`readiness_status=schema_ready_no_events/partial_events_recorded/audit_events_recorded`、message 与 recovery；required event type 必须显式覆盖 `readiness_snapshot/blocker_snapshot/manual_approval/activation_execution/post_activation_access_validation/rollback_evidence`，event status 必须显式覆盖 `planned/recorded/failed`，payload integrity status 必须显式覆盖 `payload_no_events/payload_integrity_pending/payload_integrity_failed/payload_integrity_ready`，payload issue source 必须显式覆盖 `event_type/readiness_snapshot/blocker_snapshot/review_snapshot/audit_plan_snapshot/execution_result/rollback_reference`，metadata integrity status 必须显式覆盖 `metadata_no_events/metadata_integrity_failed/metadata_integrity_ready`，metadata issue source 必须显式覆盖 `event_type/event_status/current_mode/target_mode/source`；payload integrity 必须只读校验 required event 对应 payload 为非空 JSON object，rollback evidence 必须带 rollback reference；metadata integrity 必须只读校验 event type/status、current mode、target mode 和 source；`src/lib/admin/api.ts` 必须定义 `AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationAuditReadinessStatus`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationAuditEventType`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationAuditEventStatus`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationAuditPayloadIntegrityStatus`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationAuditPayloadIssueSource`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationAuditMetadataIntegrityStatus`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationAuditMetadataIssueSource`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationAuditRequiredEvent`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationAuditPayloadIntegrityIssue`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationAuditMetadataIntegrityIssue`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationAuditRecentEvent` 和 `AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationAuditReadinessResponse`，Next proxy 和 `adminEnterpriseApi.getProjectAccessGuardActivationAuditReadiness()` 必须只读转发；Admin Enterprise Governance snapshot 必须消费 `AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardGovernanceActivationAuditReadinessStatus`、`AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardGovernanceActivationAuditPayloadIntegrityStatus` 与 `AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardGovernanceActivationAuditMetadataIntegrityStatus` 并展示 audit event count、required event type count、missing required event type count、recent event count、payload issue count、payload integrity status、metadata issue count、metadata integrity status 和 readiness status；Admin Enterprise 页面必须展示 required event coverage、payload integrity issues、metadata integrity issues 与 recent event readback。该 readiness 不得执行 activation、不写 audit 记录、不修改 `AuthorizeProjectAccess()`、不写 `projects`、不启用 enterprise_owned 授权、租户隔离或组织级 RBAC
- Admin Enterprise Project Access Guard activation manual approval evidence contract 校验，包括 GORM Admin repo 必须提供 `CreateEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationAudit(ctx, audit)` append-only 写入能力；后端必须暴露 super_admin-only `POST /api/admin/enterprise/project-access-guard-activation/manual-approval`，通过 `AdminConsoleService.RecordEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationManualApproval()` 要求 `confirm_manual_approval=true`、approval note 非空、operator admin id 非空且 activation readiness 为 `ready_to_activate`；写入事件必须为 `event_type=manual_approval`、`status=recorded`、current mode `legacy_user_owned`、target mode `enterprise_owned`，并持久化非空 JSON object 的 readiness snapshot、blocker snapshot、review snapshot 与 audit plan snapshot；`src/lib/admin/api.ts` 必须定义 `AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationManualApprovalInput/Result` 和 `adminEnterpriseApi.recordProjectAccessGuardActivationManualApproval()`；Next proxy 必须 POST 转发 `/api/admin/enterprise/project-access-guard-activation/manual-approval`；Admin Enterprise 页面必须通过命名 readiness gate 限制 super_admin 且 ready_to_activate 后才能提交，提交成功后刷新 activation readiness 与 audit readback，并展示 manual approval evidence 区块。该入口是 activation audit 受控写入入口之一，只记录人工审批审计证据，不执行 activation、不修改 `AuthorizeProjectAccess()`、不写 `projects`、不启用 enterprise_owned 授权、租户隔离或组织级 RBAC
- Admin Enterprise Project Access Guard activation execution evidence contract 校验，包括后端必须暴露 super_admin-only `POST /api/admin/enterprise/project-access-guard-activation/execution`，通过 `AdminConsoleService.RecordEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationExecution()` 要求 `confirm_activation_execution=true`、execution note 非空、operator admin id 非空、activation readiness 为 `ready_to_activate` 且 manual approval audit plan evidence 已通过 readback；写入事件必须为 `event_type=activation_execution`、`status=recorded`、current mode `legacy_user_owned`、target mode `enterprise_owned`，并持久化 readiness snapshot、blocker snapshot、review snapshot、audit plan snapshot 和非空 JSON object execution result；execution result 必须显式记录 `authorization_switch_executed=false`、`projects_written=false`、`enterprise_authorization_enabled=false`、`tenant_isolation_enabled=false` 与 `organization_rbac_enabled=false`；`src/lib/admin/api.ts` 必须定义 `AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationExecutionInput/Result` 和 `adminEnterpriseApi.recordProjectAccessGuardActivationExecution()`；Next proxy 必须 POST 转发 `/api/admin/enterprise/project-access-guard-activation/execution`；Admin Enterprise 页面必须通过命名 manual approval evidence gate 限制 super_admin、ready_to_activate 且 manual approval readback 后才能提交，提交成功后刷新 activation readiness 与 audit readback，并展示 activation execution evidence 区块。该入口只记录 execution 计划确认审计证据，不执行 activation、不修改 `AuthorizeProjectAccess()`、不写 `projects`、不启用 enterprise_owned 授权、租户隔离或组织级 RBAC
- Admin Enterprise Project Access Guard post-activation validation evidence contract 校验，包括后端必须暴露 super_admin-only `POST /api/admin/enterprise/project-access-guard-activation/post-validation`，通过 `AdminConsoleService.RecordEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardPostActivationValidation()` 要求 `confirm_post_activation_validation=true`、validation note 非空、operator admin id 非空、activation readiness 为 `ready_to_activate` 且 activation execution audit plan evidence 已通过 readback；写入事件必须为 `event_type=post_activation_access_validation`、`status=recorded`、current mode `legacy_user_owned`、target mode `enterprise_owned`，并持久化 readiness snapshot、blocker snapshot、review snapshot、audit plan snapshot 和非空 JSON object execution result；execution result 必须显式记录 `post_activation_validation_ran=false`、`authorization_switch_executed=false`、`projects_written=false`、`enterprise_authorization_enabled=false`、`tenant_isolation_enabled=false` 与 `organization_rbac_enabled=false`；`src/lib/admin/api.ts` 必须定义 `AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardPostActivationValidationInput/Result` 和 `adminEnterpriseApi.recordProjectAccessGuardPostActivationValidation()`；Next proxy 必须 POST 转发 `/api/admin/enterprise/project-access-guard-activation/post-validation`；Admin Enterprise 页面必须通过命名 activation execution evidence gate 限制 super_admin、ready_to_activate 且 activation execution readback 后才能提交，提交成功后刷新 activation readiness 与 audit readback，并展示 post-activation validation evidence 区块。该入口只记录 post-activation validation 计划确认审计证据，不执行 activation、不运行真实 post-activation access validation、不修改 `AuthorizeProjectAccess()`、不写 `projects`、不启用 enterprise_owned 授权、租户隔离或组织级 RBAC
- Admin Enterprise Project Access Guard rollback evidence contract 校验，包括后端必须暴露 super_admin-only `POST /api/admin/enterprise/project-access-guard-activation/rollback-evidence`，通过 `AdminConsoleService.RecordEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardRollbackEvidence()` 要求 `confirm_rollback_evidence=true`、rollback note 非空、rollback reference 非空、operator admin id 非空、activation readiness 为 `ready_to_activate` 且 post-activation validation audit plan evidence 已通过 readback；写入事件必须为 `event_type=rollback_evidence`、`status=recorded`、current mode `legacy_user_owned`、target mode `enterprise_owned`，并持久化 readiness snapshot、blocker snapshot、review snapshot、audit plan snapshot、非空 JSON object execution result 与非空 rollback reference；execution result 必须显式记录 `rollback_executed=false`、`post_activation_validation_ran=false`、`authorization_switch_executed=false`、`projects_written=false`、`enterprise_authorization_enabled=false`、`tenant_isolation_enabled=false` 与 `organization_rbac_enabled=false`；`src/lib/admin/api.ts` 必须定义 `AdminEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardRollbackEvidenceInput/Result` 和 `adminEnterpriseApi.recordProjectAccessGuardRollbackEvidence()`；Next proxy 必须 POST 转发 `/api/admin/enterprise/project-access-guard-activation/rollback-evidence`；Admin Enterprise 页面必须通过命名 post-activation validation evidence gate 限制 super_admin、ready_to_activate 且 post-activation validation readback 后才能提交，提交成功后刷新 activation readiness 与 audit readback，并展示 rollback evidence 区块。该入口只记录 rollback runbook 审计证据，不执行 activation、不执行真实 rollback、不运行真实 post-activation access validation、不修改 `AuthorizeProjectAccess()`、不写 `projects`、不启用 enterprise_owned 授权、租户隔离或组织级 RBAC
- Admin Enterprise Project Access Guard enterprise authorization activation contract 校验，包括 fresh init SQL 与 Go defaults 必须共同固定 `enterprise.project_access_guard.mode=legacy_user_owned` 默认值；`ProjectService.AuthorizeProjectAccess()` 必须通过 `resolveProjectAccessGuardMode()` 读取该 system_config，默认 legacy 授权，只有值为 `enterprise_owned` 时才调用 `authorizeEnterpriseOwnedProjectAccess()` 真实消费 `enterprise_project_ownerships` 与 active `enterprise_members`；enterprise active 后 switch readiness 仍可展示映射覆盖 evidence，但 `CanSwitchToEnterpriseOwned` 必须为 false。后端必须暴露 super_admin-only `POST /api/admin/enterprise/project-access-guard-activation/activate`，通过 `AdminConsoleService.ActivateEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardAuthorization()` 要求 `confirm_enterprise_authorization_activation=true`、activation note 非空、operator admin id 非空、activation readiness 为 `ready_to_activate`，且 manual approval、activation execution、post-activation validation 与 rollback evidence 均已通过 audit plan readback；服务必须写入 `system_config enterprise.project_access_guard.mode=enterprise_owned`、回读确认、在确认或 audit 写入失败时尝试恢复 previous mode，并追加 `activation_execution/recorded` audit event，execution result 必须显式记录 `authorization_switch_executed=true`、`config_key=enterprise.project_access_guard.mode`、previous/current/target mode、`projects_written=false`、`enterprise_authorization_enabled=true`、`tenant_isolation_enabled=false` 与 `organization_rbac_enabled=false`；Next proxy、Admin API contract/client 和 Admin Enterprise `admin-enterprise-project-access-guard-authorization-activation` 区块必须存在，页面必须通过 rollback evidence gate 限制提交并披露该入口只切授权模式、不写 `projects`、不启用租户隔离或组织级 RBAC。
- Admin Enterprise Project Access Guard activation audit plan evidence sync contract 校验，包括 `AdminConsoleService.GetEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationReadiness()` 必须在返回 audit plan 前只读调用 `ListEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationAudits(ctx, enterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationAuditCoverageScanLimit)`；必须通过 `readEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationAuditPlanEvidence()` 派生 `manual_approval`、`activation_execution`、`post_activation_access_validation` 与 `rollback_evidence` evidence facts；manual approval 必须通过 `isEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationManualApprovalEvidence()` 只采信合法 `manual_approval/recorded`、`ready_to_activate`、legacy-to-enterprise mode，且 readiness/blocker/review/audit plan snapshot 均为非空 JSON object 的事件；execution、post-validation 和 rollback 必须通过 `isEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationAuditRecordedEventEvidence()` 只采信 `recorded`、mode 已知且 required payload / rollback reference 通过校验的事件；必须通过 `materializeEnterpriseProjectAccessGuardActivationAuditPlanItem()` 只把对应 audit plan item 标为 `evidence_ready`，不得因一个 event 改写其他 source。该 contract 只同步只读 readiness 展示，不新增写入口、不执行 activation、不修改 `AuthorizeProjectAccess()`、不写 `projects`、不启用 enterprise_owned 授权、租户隔离或组织级 RBAC
- Enterprise Project Access Guard contract 校验，包括后端必须显式定义 `ProjectAccessGuardMode=legacy_user_owned/enterprise_owned`、`ProjectAccessDecisionStatus=granted/unauthenticated/project_not_found/forbidden/service_unavailable`、`ProjectAccessEnterpriseOwnershipShadowStatus=unavailable/no_mapping/mapping_ready/lookup_failed`、`ProjectAccessGuardEnterpriseSwitchReadinessStatus=ownership_repo_unavailable/no_projects/no_mappings/unmapped_projects/mapping_evidence_drift/enterprise_switch_ready`、`ProjectAccessDecision` 与 `ProjectAccessGuardEnterpriseSwitchReadiness`；`ProjectService.AuthorizeProjectAccess()` 必须集中当前 legacy `projects.user_id` owner guard，返回命名 decision 并通过 `HasProjectAccess()` 显式判断授权成功；`ProjectHandler.requireOwnedProject` 与 `workspace_project_access.ensureOwnedProjectAccess` 必须消费 `AuthorizeProjectAccess()` 的 decision switch，不得在入口处回退到直接 `project.UserID != uid/userID` 比较；企业归属映射只能通过 `ProjectAccessEnterpriseOwnershipRepo` 窄接口附加到 `ProjectAccessDecision.EnterpriseOwnershipShadowStatus/EnterpriseOwnership` 作为 shadow evidence，并通过 `GetProjectAccessGuardEnterpriseSwitchReadiness()` 分页比对项目真源和映射覆盖率，输出项目/映射/已映射/未映射/额外映射计数、`CanSwitchToEnterpriseOwned` 与 `EnterpriseAuthorizationActive=false`；企业授权 dry-run 只能作为附加 drift evidence，不得改变真实 `granted/forbidden`；GORM/Supabase `FindEnterpriseProjectOwnershipByProjectID()` 未命中必须返回 `nil, nil`，测试必须覆盖 owner、非 owner、缺失项目、服务不可用、缺失用户、映射存在、未映射、查询失败、非 owner 映射存在仍不授权，以及 switch readiness ready、未映射阻断、证据漂移阻断和 ownership repo 缺失。该 contract 当前不得把 `enterprise_project_ownerships` 作为访问控制事实、不得写 `projects`、不得启用租户隔离或组织级 RBAC
- Admin Prompt profile phase contract 校验，包括 `workspace-types.ts` 必须显式定义 `AdminPromptProfilePhase=Plan/Discuss/Implement/Custom`，Admin Prompt 固定 profile、保存确认 snapshot 输入与 `AdminPromptSaveConfirmationSnapshot.promptPhase` 必须直接消费该命名阶段 contract，不得回退裸 `string`；该收口不得改变 Prompt 管理页面展示、已知 profile 匹配、保存确认弹窗或运行期 Prompt 注入语义，避免 Prompt profile 阶段继续从宽字符串派生
- Admin Prompt profile copy contract 校验，包括 `workspace-types.ts` 必须显式定义 `AdminPromptProfileTitle` 与 `AdminPromptProfileDescription`，Admin Prompt 固定 profile、保存确认 snapshot 输入与 `AdminPromptSaveConfirmationSnapshot.promptTitle` 必须直接消费这些命名文案 contract，不得回退裸 `string`；该收口不得改变 Prompt 管理页面展示、已知 profile 匹配、保存确认弹窗或运行期 Prompt 注入语义，避免 Prompt profile 文案继续从宽字符串派生
- Admin System Config value type contract 校验，包括 `src/lib/admin/api.ts` 必须显式定义 `AdminSystemConfigValueType=string/number/boolean/json`，`SystemConfig.value_type`、Admin Config 保存确认 snapshot 输入与 `AdminConfigSaveConfirmationSnapshot.valueType` 必须直接消费该命名配置值类型 contract；缺失 `value_type` 必须在快照中显式表达为 `null`，不得回退为 `'string'` 或裸 `string`，该收口不得改变配置列表读取、编辑保存、权限判断或 value 解析语义，避免配置元数据类型继续漂移
- Admin System Config public flag contract 校验，包括 `src/lib/admin/api.ts` 必须显式定义 `AdminSystemConfigPublicFlag`，`SystemConfig.is_public`、Admin Config 保存确认 snapshot 输入与 `AdminConfigSaveConfirmationSnapshot.isPublicConfig` 必须直接消费该命名公开标记 contract；缺失 `is_public` 必须在快照中显式表达为 `null`，不得通过 `Boolean(...)` 压成 false 或裸 `boolean`，该收口不得改变配置列表读取、编辑保存或权限判断，避免公开标记缺失被误表达为明确非公开
- Admin System Config description contract 校验，包括 `src/lib/admin/api.ts` 必须显式定义 `AdminSystemConfigDescription`，`SystemConfig.description` 必须直接消费该命名配置说明 contract，不得回退裸 `string`；该收口不得改变配置列表读取、页面说明文案展示、编辑保存或权限判断，避免配置说明继续从宽字符串派生
- Project Mutation Dialog snapshot 校验，包括 `ProjectMutationDialogSnapshotStatus/Source` 与 `ProjectMutationDialogMode` 必须作为命名 contract 进入中央类型，`ProjectMutationDialogSnapshot` 与 `project-mutation-dialog-snapshot.tsx` helper 必须消费这些命名契约，不得通过 `ProjectMutationDialogSnapshot['status'/'source'/'mode']` 反推；`ProjectMutationDialogSnapshot` 必须覆盖 `closed/edit_ready/edit_name_missing/edit_saving/edit_failed/delete_confirming/delete_deleting/delete_failed`、来源、弹窗模式、目标项目、表单长度、应用类型、保存/删除请求、编辑/删除错误、提交/取消能力、说明、恢复建议和稳定 `project-mutation-dialog-snapshot` UI target；Project List 编辑与删除 Dialog 必须在错误 alert 和 destructive copy 前展示该快照，避免项目 mutation 状态只靠输入框、按钮禁用、保存/删除中文案或错误提示推断
- Project Edit Save Confirmation snapshot 校验，包括 `ProjectEditSaveConfirmationSnapshotStatus/Source` 与 `ProjectEditSaveConfirmationRiskLevel` 必须作为命名 contract 进入中央类型，`ProjectEditSaveConfirmationSnapshot` 与 `project-mutation-dialog-snapshot.tsx` helper 必须消费这些命名契约，不得通过 `ProjectEditSaveConfirmationSnapshot['status'/'source'/'riskLevel']` 反推；`ProjectEditSaveConfirmationSnapshot` 必须覆盖 `closed/awaiting_confirmation/confirming/save_failed/name_missing`、来源、项目 id、原名称、目标名称、原/目标应用类型、名称/描述长度、名称/描述/应用类型变更事实、保存中、错误、确认/取消能力、风险等级、说明、恢复建议和稳定 `project-edit-save-confirmation-snapshot` UI target；Project List 编辑保存按钮必须只打开结构化确认弹窗，并通过确认动作调用既有 `projectApi.update` 与本地列表 merge 链路，保存提交中必须锁定入口，避免项目元数据写入继续以编辑表单按钮直触发形式绕过 Snapshot Governance
- Project Action Confirmation Dialog snapshot 校验，包括 `ProjectActionConfirmationDialogSnapshotStatus/Source` 与 `ProjectActionConfirmationRiskLevel` 必须作为命名 contract 进入中央类型，`ProjectActionConfirmationDialogSnapshot` 与 `project-action-confirmation-dialog-snapshot.tsx` helper 必须消费这些命名契约，不得通过 `ProjectActionConfirmationDialogSnapshot['status'/'source'/'riskLevel']` 反推；`ProjectActionConfirmationDialogValue`、`hasProjectActionConfirmationDialogValue()`、`getProjectActionConfirmationDialogValue()` 与 `getProjectActionConfirmationDialogNullableValue()` 必须覆盖 action kind、project id/name、backup id、risk level、recovery 和页面弹窗文案/按钮/备份 ID fallback；`ProjectActionConfirmationDialogSnapshot` 必须覆盖 `closed/awaiting_confirmation/confirming`、来源、受控 action kind、项目、备份、确认中状态、项目/备份上下文完整性、确认/取消能力、风险等级、说明、恢复建议和稳定 `project-action-confirmation-dialog-snapshot` UI target；Project List 停止运行时、软删除恢复、资源告警事件创建、资源告警 webhook 通知发送、资源告警硬配额执行、手动创建备份、自动备份策略执行、远端备份上传、远端完整备份下载导入、本地备份恢复和远端备份恢复必须统一打开结构化 React Dialog，并通过 `handleConfirmProjectAction` 分发到既有确认执行函数，确认后仍只提交 `stop_container / delete_restore / confirm_create / confirm_send / confirm_execute / create_backup / automatic_run / remote_upload / remote_download / confirm_restore` 后端 guard；`src/app/projects/page.tsx` 不得重新引入 `window.confirm`，停止运行时、软删除恢复、远端上传和远端下载导入按钮也不得回退为直接调用写入副作用；受控操作弹窗的标题、描述、项目、操作、备份、确认按钮文案、卡片 busy 项目 ID 和 backupId 分发门禁必须先派生 `hasPendingProjectActionConfirmation`、`pendingProjectActionProjectId`、`pendingProjectActionTitle`、`pendingProjectActionDescription`、`pendingProjectActionProjectLabel`、`pendingProjectActionKindLabel`、`pendingProjectActionBackupId`、`shouldRenderPendingProjectActionBackupId`、`pendingProjectActionConfirmLabel` 与 `confirmationBackupId` 命名 facts，不得回退 `confirmation?.kind/backupId/riskLevel/recovery`、`confirmation?.project.project_id/name`、`pendingProjectActionConfirmation?.title/description/kind/backupId/confirmLabel`、`pendingProjectActionConfirmation?.project.project_id/name`、`confirmation.project.name` inline OR fallback 或 `&& confirmation.backupId` truthy 分支，避免高风险操作确认状态、风险边界和恢复建议落回浏览器原生弹窗、JSX inline fallback 或不可观测直触发而脱离 Snapshot Governance

- Project mutation/action confirmation snapshot strip display gates 校验，包括 `ProjectMutationDialogSnapshotStrip`、`ProjectEditSaveConfirmationSnapshotStrip` 与 `ProjectActionConfirmationDialogSnapshotStrip` 中的项目、原项目名和备份 ID 必须先派生 `projectIdLabel`、`originalProjectNameLabel` 与 `backupIdLabel` 命名 label facts，再在 JSX 中消费这些 facts 展示 `none` fallback；不得回退这些 `snapshot.* || 'none'` inline fallback，避免项目编辑/删除和项目受控操作确认快照的目标身份观测重新落回 JSX 内隐式 OR fallback
- Git Branch Mutation Confirmation snapshot 校验，包括 `GitBranchMutationConfirmationSnapshot` 必须覆盖 `closed/awaiting_confirmation/confirming`、来源、action、目标分支、新分支名、当前分支、上下文完整性、当前分支阻断、确认/取消能力、风险等级、说明、恢复建议和稳定 `workspace-git-branch-mutation-confirmation-snapshot` UI target；pending confirmation 必须消费命名 `GitBranchMutationConfirmationAction`；Workspace 桌面端和移动端 Git 面板的目标本地分支删除、重命名必须统一打开结构化 AlertDialog，并通过 `handleConfirmBranchMutation` 分发到既有删除/重命名动作，确认后仍只提交 guarded `git branch -d` / `git branch -m` 后端链路；按钮不得重新直接调用 `onDeleteGitBranch(compareTargetValue)` 或 `onRenameGitBranch(compareTargetValue, branchRenameValue)`，避免高风险 Git 分支写操作确认状态、当前分支 guard 和恢复建议落回不可观测直触发
- Git Branch Compare File Apply Confirmation snapshot 校验，包括 `GitBranchCompareFileApplyConfirmationSnapshot` 必须覆盖 `closed/awaiting_confirmation/confirming`、来源、action、base branch、head branch、file path、上下文完整性、same-branch guard、确认/取消能力、风险等级、说明、恢复建议和稳定 `workspace-git-branch-compare-file-apply-confirmation-snapshot` UI target；Workspace 桌面端和移动端 Git branch compare 文件行的“引入此文件”必须统一打开结构化 AlertDialog，并通过 `handleConfirmBranchCompareFileApply` 分发到既有 branch compare file apply 动作，确认后仍只提交 guarded checkout 后端链路；按钮不得重新直接调用 `onApplyGitBranchCompareFile(gitBranchCompare.base_branch, gitBranchCompare.head_branch, file.path)`，避免路径限定 checkout 单文件引入的分支上下文、dirty path guard 和恢复建议落回不可观测直触发
- Git Tag Mutation Confirmation snapshot 校验，包括 `GitTagMutationConfirmationSnapshot` 必须覆盖 `closed/awaiting_confirmation/confirming`、来源、action、目标标签、目标提交、标签上下文完整性、确认/取消能力、风险等级、说明、恢复建议和稳定 `workspace-git-tag-mutation-confirmation-snapshot` UI target；pending confirmation 必须消费命名 `GitTagMutationConfirmationAction`；Workspace 桌面端和移动端 Git 面板的本地标签删除必须统一打开结构化 AlertDialog，并通过 `handleConfirmTagMutation` 分发到既有删除动作，确认后仍只提交 guarded `git tag -d` 后端链路；按钮不得重新直接调用 `onDeleteGitTag(tag.name)`，避免高风险 Git 标签写操作确认状态、只删本地标签边界和恢复建议落回不可观测直触发
- Git Remote Branch Refresh Confirmation snapshot 校验，包括 `GitRemoteBranchRefreshConfirmationSnapshot` 必须覆盖 `closed/awaiting_confirmation/confirming`、来源、action、remote 名称、remote 上下文完整性、确认/取消能力、风险等级、说明、恢复建议和稳定 `workspace-git-remote-branch-refresh-confirmation-snapshot` UI target；Workspace 桌面端和移动端 Git 面板的 remote refs refresh Enter 与按钮必须统一打开结构化 AlertDialog，并通过 `handleConfirmRemoteBranchRefresh` 分发到既有 remote refs refresh 动作，确认后仍只提交 controlled `git fetch <remote>` 后端链路；按钮和 Enter 不得重新直接调用 `onRefreshGitRemoteBranches(remoteBranchRefreshValue)`，避免网络与 Git refs 写操作确认状态、remote guard 和恢复建议落回不可观测直触发
- Git Remote Branch Create Confirmation snapshot 校验，包括 `GitRemoteBranchCreateConfirmationSnapshot` 必须覆盖 `closed/awaiting_confirmation/confirming`、来源、action、remote branch、本地分支、remote/local 上下文完整性、确认/取消能力、风险等级、说明、恢复建议和稳定 `workspace-git-remote-branch-create-confirmation-snapshot` UI target；Workspace 桌面端和移动端 Git 面板的 remote branch 本地 tracking branch 创建 Enter 与按钮必须统一打开结构化 AlertDialog，并通过 `handleConfirmRemoteBranchCreate` 分发到既有 create-from-remote 动作，确认后仍只提交 guarded `git branch --track <name> refs/remotes/<remote_branch>` 后端链路；按钮和 Enter 不得重新直接调用 `onCreateGitBranchFromRemote(branch.name, localBranchName)`，避免 Git refs 写操作确认状态、remote ref guard、本地分支 guard 和恢复建议落回不可观测直触发
- Git Commit File Restore Confirmation snapshot 校验，包括 `GitCommitFileRestoreConfirmationSnapshot` 必须覆盖 `closed/awaiting_confirmation/confirming`、来源、action、commit、file path、commit/path 上下文完整性、确认/取消能力、风险等级、说明、恢复建议和稳定 `workspace-git-commit-file-restore-confirmation-snapshot` UI target；Workspace 桌面端和移动端 Git commit diff 文件行的“恢复此文件”必须统一打开结构化 AlertDialog，并通过 `handleConfirmCommitFileRestore` 分发到既有 commit file restore 动作，确认后仍只提交 guarded checkout 单文件恢复后端链路；按钮不得重新直接调用 `onRestoreCommitFile(selectedCommit, gitDiffFileItemSnapshot.path)` 或 `onRestoreCommitFile(commit, gitDiffFileItemSnapshot.path)`，避免历史版本恢复的 commit 证据、dirty path guard 和恢复建议落回不可观测直触发
- Workspace Commit Restore / Dirty Close / Explorer Confirmation snapshot 校验，包括 `WorkspaceCommitRestoreConfirmationSnapshotStatus/Source/Action`、`WorkspaceDirtyCloseConfirmationSnapshotStatus/Source/Action`、`WorkspaceExplorerOperationConfirmationSnapshotStatus/Source/Action` 与各 risk level 必须作为命名 contract 进入中央类型，相关 helper 必须消费这些命名契约，不得通过 indexed access 反推；commit hash/message/author trim、dirty file、Explorer target/input/source path 与 yes/no display label 必须先派生 `getWorkspaceCommitRestoreConfirmationTrimmedValue()`、`getWorkspaceDirtyCloseConfirmationSnapshotLabel()`、`getWorkspaceDirtyCloseConfirmationSnapshotBooleanLabel()`、`getWorkspaceExplorerOperationConfirmationSnapshotLabel()`、`getWorkspaceExplorerOperationConfirmationSnapshotBooleanLabel()`、`shortHash`、`fileNameLabel`、`filePathLabel`、`targetPathLabel`、`inputNameLabel`、`nodePathLabel`、`hasMessageLabel`、`canConfirmLabel` 与 `canCancelLabel` 命名 facts，不得回退 `value?.trim() ?? ''`、`commit?.hash?.trim() || ''`、`commitHash || null`、`commitHash ? normalizeCommitVersion(commitHash) : 'none'`、`snapshot.fileName ?? 'none'`、`snapshot.targetPath ?? 'none'` 或 JSX inline `snapshot.canConfirm ? 'yes' : 'no'`；Workspace 页面 overlay 的“确认回到该版本”、Dirty close 与 Explorer 文件事务必须展示对应 snapshot，并通过 snapshot `canConfirm/canCancel` 控制确认和取消按钮，确认后仍只调用既有恢复、dirty close 或 Explorer context operation 链路；不得回退为只靠 AlertDialog 文案、`isRestoringCommit`、hash 字符串、dirty 文件名或散装 Explorer 风险文案推断确认状态，避免全工作区回滚、dirty buffer 和文件事务的目标、风险边界和恢复建议脱离 Snapshot Governance
- Workspace page overlay confirmation 显式门禁校验，包括 Explorer 文件事务、dirty close、整仓 commit restore 与 Header 清空对话确认必须统一使用显式 pending/open/canConfirm/canCancel 门禁；Explorer 文件事务 handler 必须显式区分 `pendingExplorerOperation === null` 与 `explorerOperationConfirmationSnapshot.canConfirm !== true`，Explorer/dirty close/commit restore 打开态必须使用 pending `!== null`，关闭态必须使用 `open === false` 或 `open === false && isRestoringCommit === false`，确认/取消按钮 disabled 必须使用 `canConfirm/canCancel === false`，dirty close 丢弃与保存并关闭 handler 必须复核 snapshot 能力；整仓 commit restore 与 Header 清空对话的 `AlertDialogAction` 必须先 `event.preventDefault()`，再按 snapshot `canConfirm === true` 调用既有 handler，不得回退 `!!pending`、truthy `canConfirm/canCancel`、隐式 `!open` 或异步确认前默认关闭确认弹窗，避免文件事务、dirty buffer 丢弃、整仓恢复和本地 clear chat 确认边界脱离 Snapshot Governance
- Git Worktree Commit Confirmation snapshot 校验，包括 `GitWorktreeCommitConfirmationSnapshot` 必须覆盖 `closed/awaiting_confirmation/confirming`、来源、action、commit message、message length、当前分支、dirty file count、message/dirty 完整性、确认/取消能力、风险等级、说明、恢复建议和稳定 `workspace-git-worktree-commit-confirmation-snapshot` UI target；Workspace 桌面端和移动端 Git 面板的 worktree commit Enter 与提交按钮必须统一打开结构化 AlertDialog，并通过 `handleConfirmWorktreeCommit` 分发到既有 commit 动作，确认后仍只提交既有显式 POST / guarded worktree commit 后端链路；按钮和 Enter 不得重新直接调用 `onCommitWorktree(worktreeCommitMessageValue)`，避免 `git add -A` / `git commit -m` 全量 dirty 提交的确认状态、dirty 证据和恢复建议落回不可观测直触发
- Workspace Git mutation confirmation 显式门禁校验，包括桌面端与移动端 Git 面板的分支对比单文件引入、提交文件恢复、本地分支创建/删除/重命名、分支切换、remote refs 刷新、remote tracking branch 创建、stash apply、worktree commit 和单文件 discard 确认弹窗必须统一使用 pending confirmation `!== null` 作为打开态，handler 必须显式区分 pending `=== null` 与 snapshot `canConfirm !== true`，onOpenChange 必须用 `open === false && isConfirming... === false` 控制取消关闭，确认/取消按钮 disabled 必须使用 `canConfirm/canCancel === false`；不得回退 `!!pending`、truthy pending、truthy `canConfirm/canCancel` 或隐式 `!open`，避免 Git 受控写操作确认边界在桌面/移动端之间漂移

branch.branch`；桌面端与移动端 `getGitPanelCurrentBranchName()`、`getGitPanelCompareTargetBranches()` 与 `getGitPanelObservedRemoteNames()` 的 helper 内部必须使用显式 `for...of` scan 和 Set-backed 去重物化，不得回退 `branches.find(...)`、`branches.filter(...)`、`remotes.map(...)`、`remoteBranches.map(...)`、remote trim/filter 链或 `Array.from(new Set(...))` 数据回调管线；branch switch blocked 工程状态文案必须通过 `getGitBranchSwitchTargetBranchLabel()` 命名 display fact 派生 target branch，不得回退 `readiness.target_branch` inline fallback；不得回退 current branch optional fallback、`hasCurrentBranchName === true && currentBranchName !== null`、compare target nested ternary、remote refs OR fallback、worktree dirty/message inline gate、remote local-name OR fallback、`gitBranchSwitchReadiness &&`、`hasBranchSwitchReadiness === true && gitBranchSwitchReadiness !== null`、`renderableBranchSwitchReadiness !== null &&`、JSX `can_switch` 三元或 current/target branch inline fallback，避免 Git 分支目标按钮、remote refs 刷新、worktree commit 和 readiness 证据重新依赖 truthy 判断或数组回调数据物化

'unknown'`、`summary.restartRuntimeAction?.reasonCode || ''`、`summary.restartRuntimeAction?.label || null`、`summary.restartRuntimeAction?.description || null` 或 `summary.restartRuntimeAction?.reasonCode || null`，避免恢复运行时确认原因和 snapshot 输入重新依赖 inline OR/nullish fallback
- Workspace Git confirmation dialog render fact 校验，包括桌面端与移动端 Git 面板的分支对比单文件引入、提交文件恢复、本地分支创建/删除/重命名、分支切换、remote refs 刷新、remote tracking branch 创建、stash apply、worktree commit 和单文件 discard 确认弹窗描述必须先通过 `getGitConfirmationDialogLabel()` 和 `branchCompareApplyHeadBranchLabel`、`branchCompareApplyFilePathLabel`、`branchCompareApplyBaseBranchLabel`、`commitFileRestoreFilePathLabel`、`tagMutationTagNameLabel`、`branchMutationBranchNameLabel`、`branchMutationNextBranchNameLabel`、`branchSwitchCurrentBranchDialogLabel`、`branchSwitchTargetBranchDialogLabel`、`remoteBranchRefreshRemoteNameLabel`、`remoteBranchCreateRemoteBranchNameLabel`、`remoteBranchCreateLocalBranchNameLabel`、`stashMutationStashRefLabel`、`worktreeCommitDirtyFilesLabel`、`worktreeCommitCurrentBranchLabel` 与 `worktreeFileDiscardFilePathLabel` 显式 facts 派生显示值；确认弹窗 title、description、confirm button label 与 branch mutation risk variant 必须通过 `getGitConfirmationButtonLabel()`、`getGitTagMutationAction()`、`isGitTagMutationCreateAction()`、`getGitTagMutationTitle()`、`getGitTagMutationDescription()`、`getGitTagMutationConfirmLabel()`、`getGitBranchMutationAction()`、`isGitBranchMutationCreateAction()`、`isGitBranchMutationDeleteAction()`、`getGitBranchMutationTitle()`、`getGitBranchMutationDescription()`、`getGitBranchMutationConfirmLabel()` 与 `getGitConfirmationButtonVariant()` 命名 facts/readers 派生；不得回退 `pending... || 'unknown'`、`pendingWorktreeCommitConfirmation?.dirtyFiles || 0`、`confirmation !== null && confirmation.action === ...`、JSX 内联 pending action 三元、confirming label 三元或 risk level 三元，避免用户点击确认前看到的高风险 Git 操作文案、按钮文案和风险按钮 tone 重新依赖 JSX inline fallback

- Workspace Git commit row snapshot strip display gate 校验，包括 Git Commit Detail、Commit Item 与 Diff File Item 快照的 Message、Author、Email、Time、StaleDetail、InspectDiff、Selected、View、PathOk、Content、HasAdd、HasDel 和 Expand 展示必须先通过 `getGitSnapshotBooleanLabel()` 派生命名 display labels，再进入 JSX；不得回退 `snapshot.hasMessage ? 'yes' : 'no'`、`snapshot.hasAuthor ? 'yes' : 'no'`、`snapshot.hasEmail ? 'yes' : 'no'`、`snapshot.hasTime ? 'yes' : 'no'`、`snapshot.hasStaleDetail ? 'yes' : 'no'`、`snapshot.canInspectDiff ? 'yes' : 'no'`、`snapshot.isSelected ? 'yes' : 'no'`、`snapshot.canView ? 'yes' : 'no'`、`snapshot.hasPath ? 'yes' : 'no'`、`snapshot.hasContent ? 'yes' : 'no'`、`snapshot.hasAdditions ? 'yes' : 'no'`、`snapshot.hasDeletions ? 'yes' : 'no'` 或 `snapshot.canExpand ? 'yes' : 'no'` inline display gate，避免 Git 提交详情、提交行和 diff 文件行重新依赖 JSX truthy display gate 拼接用户可见状态
- Workspace Editor/Explorer builder display fallback 校验，包括 Editor Panel、Explorer Panel 与 Explorer 文件事务确认快照的 buffer status/operation fallback 必须先通过 `getEditorPanelBufferStatusLabel()`、`getExplorerPanelBufferStatusLabel()` 与 `getWorkspaceExplorerOperationConfirmationOperationLabel()` 派生命名 facts；不得回退 `editorBufferStatus?.status ?? 'none'`、`activeFileBufferStatus?.status ?? 'none'` 或 `operation ?? 'none'` inline nullish fallback，避免 editor/explorer 快照 builder 重新通过散装 fallback 拼接用户可见状态
- Workspace Git confirmation snapshot helper 显式门禁校验，包括共享 Git confirmation snapshot 派生器中的分支对比单文件引入、提交文件恢复、本地分支创建/删除/重命名、分支切换、标签创建/删除、remote refs 刷新、remote tracking branch 创建、stash apply、worktree commit 和单文件 discard 必须先派生 `hasConfirmation = confirmation !== null`、命名 `has... = value !== null && value.length > 0`、显式 `isSameBranch/isCurrentBranch/readinessAllowsSwitch` 事实，再用 `hasConfirmation === true`、`has... === true`、`is... === false` 与 `isConfirming === false` 组合 `canConfirm/canCancel`；不得回退 `Boolean(confirmation)`、`Boolean(value)`、`Boolean(confirmation?.canSwitch)`、`&& !isConfirming`、`&& !isSameBranch` 或 `&& !isCurrentBranch`，避免共享 snapshot helper 自身仍以隐式 truthy/取反派生 action 能力
- Workspace Git read-only snapshot helper 显式 presence 门禁校验，包括共享 Git panel、branch、remote branch、worktree、branch compare、commit detail、commit item 与 diff file item snapshot helper 的存在性、元数据完整性、stale detail、查看能力和展开能力必须通过命名 `has.../can...` 事实派生；selected/compare/worktree 对象存在性必须使用 `!== null`，字符串存在性必须使用 `.length > 0`，组合能力必须使用 `=== true/false`，不得回退 `Boolean(...)`、`find(Boolean)`、`filter(Boolean)`、`!selectedCommit` 或 `!file.path`，避免只读 Git snapshot 状态继续通过 truthy predicate 或隐式取反判断
- Workspace non-Git confirmation snapshot helper 显式门禁校验，包括 Workspace commit restore、Explorer operation、dirty close、附件移除、停止生成、清空对话、Terminal close 与 Runtime Health recovery confirmation helper 必须先派生命名 `has.../can...` 事实，再用 `!== null`、`.length > 0` 与 `=== true/false` 组合打开态、目标存在性、提交中状态、后端同步能力和确认/取消能力；Commit Restore 的 commit hash/message presence 与 author/hash label fallback 必须先派生 `hasWorkspaceCommitRestoreConfirmationTextValue()`、`hasCommitHash`、`hasCommit`、`hasMessage`、`authorLabel` 与 `commitHashLabel`；Explorer operation 的 label/input/target path presence 与 target/input/node path label fallback 必须先派生 `hasWorkspaceExplorerOperationConfirmationTextValue()`、`hasLabel`、`hasInputName`、`hasTargetPath`、`targetPathLabel`、`inputNameLabel` 与 `nodePathLabel`，不得回退 `commitHash !== null && commitHash.length > 0`、`commitMessage !== null && commitMessage.length > 0`、`label.length > 0`、`normalizedInputName.length > 0`、`normalizedTargetPath !== null && normalizedTargetPath.length > 0`、`value !== null && value !== undefined && value.length > 0`、`Boolean(...)`、`filter(Boolean)`、`&& !isConfirming`、`&& !isSubmitting`、`&& !isRestoring`、`!hasTargetPath` 或 `!requiresTargetPath`，避免非 Git confirmation snapshot helper 继续通过 truthy predicate、direct nullable length check 或隐式取反判断 action 能力

summary、Foundation panel、Engineering state panel 与 Debug context snapshot helper 必须先派生命名 `has.../can...` 事实，再用 `!== null`、`!== undefined`、`.length > 0` 与 `=== true/false` 表达项目、文件、消息、导航目标、恢复动作、面板能力和引导状态；Project Bootstrap 的 route/project text presence 与 ProjectId/Name label fallback 必须先派生 null && value !== undefined && value.length > 0`、`Boolean(...)`、`filter(Boolean)`、`find(Boolean)`、`!hasProject`、`!hasActiveFile`、`!foundationState`、`!hasAllRequiredDrafts` 或 `canRestore && canView`，避免 - Project List snapshot helper 显式 presence/action 门禁校验，包括 Project List page、Project card、Project mutation dialog、Project edit save confirmation 与 Project action confirmation snapshot helper 必须先派生命名 `has.../can...` 事实，再用 `!== null`、`.length > 0` 与 `=== true/false` 表达错误、notice、目标项目、删除恢复、备份上下文、提交中状态和确认/取消能力；不得回退 `Boolean(...)`、`filter(Boolean)`、`find(Boolean)`、`&& !isSaving`、`&& !isDeleting`、`&& !isConfirming`、`!hasProject`、`!requiresBackup` 或 `!isOpen`，避免项目列表、卡片、编辑/删除确认和高风险项目操作 snapshot helper 继续通过 truthy predicate 或隐式取反判断状态与 action 能力

- Admin Config/Prompt/Template snapshot helper 显式 presence/action/display 门禁校验，包括 Admin Config、Admin Prompt 与 Admin Template page/save confirmation snapshot helper 必须先派生命名 `has.../can...` 事实，再用 `!== null`、`.length > 0` 与 `=== true/false` 表达加载/保存错误、编辑目标、保存确认弹窗、稳定 profile、配置 key、确认/取消能力和风险等级；配置类页面与保存确认快照的 editing/key/profile/title/type/public fallback 与 yes-no 展示必须先派生 `getAdminConfigSnapshotLabel()`、`getAdminConfigPublicFlagLabel()`、`getAdminPromptSnapshotLabel()`、`getAdminTemplateSnapshotLabel()`、`editingKeyLabel`、`configKeyLabel`、`promptKeyLabel`、`templateKeyLabel`、`isPublicConfigLabel`、`isKnownPromptLabel`、`isKnownTemplateLabel`、`isClearingOverrideLabel` 及对应 display labels；不得回退 `Boolean(...)`、`filter(Boolean)`、`find(Boolean)`、`!loading`、`!saving`、`!editingKey`、`!isOpen`、`!hasConfig`、`!hasPrompt`、`!hasTemplate`、`!canEdit`、`snapshot.editingKey || 'none'`、`snapshot.configKey || 'none'`、`profile?.title || ...`、`snapshot.valueType ?? 'missing'`、`snapshot.isPublicConfig === null ? ...` 或 JSX inline `snapshot.* ? 'yes' : 'no'`，避免 Admin 配置类 snapshot helper 继续通过 truthy predicate、隐式取反或 inline fallback 判断编辑、保存和展示能力

- Admin LLM Provider runtime summary/card materializer 校验，包括 Admin LLM Providers 页面必须通过 `buildAdminLLMProviderRuntimeSummary()` 显式 `for...of` reader 派生 enabled/loaded/active/drift 运行态统计，并通过 `materializeAdminLLMProviderCardNodes()` 显式 `for...of` node materializer 渲染 provider card；provider card materializer 必须继续传递 runtime mutation、edit 和 delete confirmation handlers；不得回退 `providers.filter(...)`、`providers.find(...)` 或 `providers.map(...)` inline array pipeline，避免 Provider 统计和卡片节点重新通过匿名数组回调派生。
- Admin diagnostics snapshot/card filtered-empty, content and error gates 校验，包括 Admin Capability Preflight、Provider Health 与 Runtime Health diagnostics snapshot 的 list empty/filtered-empty 判断必须先通过 `getAdminCapabilityPreflightDiagnosticsItemCount()`、`hasAdminCapabilityPreflightDiagnosticsItems()`、`getAdminProviderHealthDiagnosticsProviderSummaryCount()`、`shouldRenderAdminProviderHealthDiagnosticsFilteredEmpty()`、`getAdminRuntimeHealthDiagnosticsProjectCount()` 与 `shouldRenderAdminRuntimeHealthDiagnosticsFilteredEmpty()` 命名 facts 派生；Admin Runtime Health、Provider Health 与 Audit diagnostics card 的主体内容渲染必须先通过 `shouldRenderAdminRuntimeHealthDiagnosticsContent()`、`shouldRenderAdminProviderHealthDiagnosticsContent()` 与 `shouldRenderAdminAuditDiagnosticsContent()` 命名 facts 派生；diagnostic link copy error 与 URL sync error 展示必须先通过 `shouldRenderAdmin*DiagnosticLinkCopyError()` 与 `shouldRenderAdmin*DiagnosticUrlSyncError()` 命名 facts 派生；不得回退 `providerPreflight.items.length === 0`、`hasActiveFilters === true && summary.providerSummaries.length === 0`、`hasActiveFilters === true && summary.projects.length === 0`、`summary.totalProjectCount > 0 ?`、`summary.totalProviderCount > 0 ?`、`unfilteredSummary.totalLogCount > 0 ?`、`diagnosticLinkCopyError ?` 或 `diagnosticUrlSyncError ?`，避免 Admin diagnostics snapshot/card 的空态、筛选空态、主体内容和诊断错误提示重新依赖裸 list length、inline total-count ternary 或 nullable error ternary 判断
- Admin diagnostics card display label facts 校验，包括 Admin Runtime Health diagnostics card 的 running/blocked badge tone、empty/healthy message、filter option label 与 diagnostic link copy button label 必须先通过 `getAdminRuntimeHealthRunningBadgeTone()`、`getAdminRuntimeHealthBlockedBadgeTone()`、`getAdminRuntimeHealthEmptyMessage()`、`getAdminRuntimeHealthHealthyMessage()`、`getAdminRuntimeHealthFilterOptionLabel()` 与 `getAdminRuntimeHealthDiagnosticLinkCopyActionLabel()` 命名 display facts 派生；Admin Provider Health diagnostics card 的 drift/blocked badge tone、empty/healthy message、filter option label、diagnostic link copy button label 与 priority provider identity/runtime label 必须先通过 `getAdminProviderHealthDriftBadgeTone()`、`getAdminProviderHealthBlockedBadgeTone()`、`getAdminProviderHealthEmptyMessage()`、`getAdminProviderHealthHealthyMessage()`、`getAdminProviderHealthFilterOptionLabel()`、`getAdminProviderHealthDiagnosticLinkCopyActionLabel()`、`getAdminProviderHealthPriorityIdentityLabel()` 与 `getAdminProviderHealthPriorityRuntimeLabel()` 命名 display facts 派生；Admin Audit diagnostics card 的 action badge tone、empty message 与 diagnostic link copy button label 必须先通过 `getAdminAuditActionBadgeTone()`、`getAdminAuditEmptyMessage()` 与 `getAdminAuditDiagnosticLinkCopyActionLabel()` 命名 display facts 派生；不得回退 `summary.runningCount > 0 ?`、`summary.blockedCount > 0 ?`、`summary.driftCount > 0 ?`、`summary.actionCount > 0 ?`、`summary.totalProjectCount === 0 ?`、`summary.totalProviderCount === 0 ?`、`unfilteredSummary.totalLogCount === 0 ?`、`summary.healthyMessage || undefined`、`option === 'all' ?`、`diagnosticLinkCopied ?`、`provider.isDefault ?` 或 `provider.isRuntimeLoaded ?`，避免 Runtime/Provider/Audit 三张诊断卡重新通过 inline ternary、OR fallback 或 JSX 局部分支派生用户可见 badge、空态/健康态、筛选 option、复制按钮和 provider priority 标签
- Auth page snapshot helper 显式 presence/action 门禁校验，包括普通用户 Auth page snapshot helper 必须先派生命名 `has.../can...` 事实，再用 `!== null`、`.length > 0` 与 `=== true/false` 表达认证错误、auth storage notice、邮箱/密码/用户名完整性、认证检查、已登录跳转、提交中、提交/切换/返回首页能力和状态来源；不得回退 `Boolean(...)`、`filter(Boolean)`、`find(Boolean)`、`!authLoading`、`!isAuthenticated`、`!isSubmitting`、`!canSubmit`、`&& !...`、`|| !...` 或 `!has...`，避免 Auth 页面 snapshot helper 继续通过 truthy predicate 或隐式取反判断认证 readiness 与 action 能力

- Admin Layout snapshot 与 session role contract 校验，包括 `src/lib/admin/api.ts` 必须显式定义 `AdminSessionRole=admin/super_admin/unknown` 与 `AdminProfile`，`AdminProfileRawResponse` 与 `AdminLoginRawResponse` 必须显式列出 login/profile raw 字段并禁止通过 `Omit<...>` 从 normalized response 反推；`adminAuthApi.login()/getProfile()` 和 `admin_profile` cache 读取必须把后端 raw `role` 归一为稳定枚举并保留 `raw_role`；`AdminLayoutSnapshotStatus` 必须显式覆盖 `login_route/checking/token_missing_redirect/token_read_failed_redirect/cached_profile_ready/profile_cache_read_failed/profile_cache_write_failed/profile_cache_url_cleanup_failed/profile_verified/profile_verification_failed_redirect/unauthenticated/ready`，`AdminLayoutSnapshotSource` 必须显式覆盖 `route/token_storage/profile_cache/profile_api/browser_history/admin_session`，`AdminSessionSnapshotRole` 必须显式覆盖 `none/admin/super_admin/unknown`，`AdminLayoutNavHref=/admin|/admin/llm|/admin/prompts|/admin/templates|/admin/config|/admin/audit|/admin/users|/admin/admins|/admin/roles|/admin/enterprise` 与 `AdminLayoutNavLabelKey=dashboard/llm/prompts/templates/config/audit/users/admins/roles/enterprise` 必须进入中央类型并由 `NavItem.href/labelKey` 直接消费；`AdminLayoutSnapshot` 必须消费这些稳定契约并覆盖 pathname、checking、authed、profile、角色、raw 角色、权限数量、可见导航数量、storage notice、children/logout/nav 能力、说明、恢复建议和稳定 `admin-layout-snapshot` UI target；Admin Layout state 必须消费 `AdminLayoutSnapshotStatus` 与 `AdminProfileCache`，角色标签 helper 必须消费 `AdminRoleLabelProfile` 且不得通过 `Pick<AdminProfileCache, role/raw_role>` 反推输入，不得通过 `ReturnType<typeof buildAdminLayoutSnapshot>` 反推 builder 返回结构，也不得用宽字符串 session role 直接授予 super_admin 导航能力，导航 href 不得回退到宽 `string`；Admin Layout loading 分支和后台 shell 必须展示该快照，避免后台外壳鉴权状态只靠 loading 文案、storage banner、导航显隐或 router.replace/push 副作用推断
